Duration    8-10 Semesters 
Semester duration  15-18 weeks  
The State of Jammu and Kashmir is extremely rich in 
wildlife resources and several threats are operating to 
deplete these resources without any sustainable 
development. Accordingly, some wild animals have 
become threatened which if not protected would soon 
become extinct. There is an intense need to explore 
these precious resources to manage them for their 
sustainable utilization to uplift the socioeconomic 
conditions of the local communities. Keeping in view the 
national and global trends the department has currently 
initiated the BS-4 year degree program in Zoology. The 
department admits under graduate and graduate 
students for BS-4 Year, M.Phil. and Ph.D. degree 
programs in Zoology.

and Toxicology.

emphasis stems from the fact that in this field there are 
yet a great number of phenomena that remains 
unexplained and unexplored and unexplored.

satisfaction.

Dear Students
Welcome to the Faculty of Arts at the University of Azad 
Jammu and Kashmir. You will find that this is a diverse and 
inclusive faculty where you will thrive and experience 
transformational change as you pursue studies in the 
social sciences, humanities or fine arts.
Our primary purpose is to guide and assist our students to 
hone research skills, aesthetic values and social 
sensibilities that equip them to thrive with intelligence and 
integrity. We look forward to helping you accomplish your 
goals and aspirations, and I invite you to explore the 
opportunities available in the Faculty of Arts.
Prof. Dr. Haroon ur Rasheed
At the Faculty of Arts, you can create your own path. 
Choose the faculty that puts you in control. Choose Arts.
Arts is an education in people, explored through countless 
perspectives. It is a hub where the best minds come 
together to take on complex modern problems. Arts act as 
a catalyst in shaping the changing world. Nothing about 
our Arts degrees is ordinary. We offer a wide range of 
undergraduate and postgraduate programs across 15 
disciplines. We have a committed group of researchers 
and academicians with expertise in English language and 
literature, sociology, psychology, Islamic studies, business 
studies, law, Kashmir studies, education, economics,

Communication.

The Department of Art & Design was started as a University 
College of Textile Designing for women in 1982. It was a 
progressive idea in the context of Higher Education AJK which 
was meant to provide advanced options of professional 
development for merchandized segment of society at that 
time. The B.F.A (four year) classes were started in 2005 and 
disciplines of visual 'Communication Design Textile Designing 
and Painting have been Introduced. The BFA program is being 
conducted under the Semester system comprising eight 
semesters. A unique feature of this program is that it 
represents a balanced blend of classical and modern trends, 
theoretical and practical knowledge related to the field of Art 
and Design, the following three disciplines have been 
introduced; 
Ÿ· Textile Design
Ÿ· Visual Communication Design
The Department of Art and Design welcomes students who 
wish to collaborate with their immediate areas as well as 
embrace regional or world-wide opportunities in various fields 
of art. Diverse subjects graphic, digital arts, digital designs, 
textile design, printing, fashion designing , painting , 
photograph, sculpture- art histories through a range of 
classes are taught by recognized experts, designers and 
artists. The Department of Art & Design has great potential to 
expand in diverse fields/ subjects which are of great relevance 
to the present day needs of the tob market. It has been set up 
with an aim to provide the students with the best academic life 
to meet the challenges of practical life in the relevant field of 
profession.
Students Resources  
Art & Design students have considerable access to 
the facilities they need to create, learn and excel in 
Ÿ Painting

Cultural Celebrations 
Students of Art and Design used to participate 
on national level for different activities and 
professional tasks which enhanced their 
confidence and provide them chance to get 
practical experience in market. During course 
of studies students -used to in touch as 
internees with Textile mills, advertising 
agencies, Media, art galleries, cultural 
academies and Govt. Departments: small 
industries, Tourism archaeology. This 
department is also organizing the workshops 
& seminars in collaboration with AJK TAVTA, 
ECO & PNCA. Department of Art & Design has 
signed M.O.U with MINTEX (Ministry of Textile 
Industry, GOP) to facilitate the local 
Commercial Linkages  
Dept. of Art and Design celebrates various 
cultural events with their own uniqueness and 
way of expressions combined with an 
awareness and respect of local values and 
traditions. Theses Cultural events help to 
strengthen the intellectual environment and 
support youth commitment for the promotion 
of national 'identity. The University involved 
other AJK Govt. Departments for the 
promotion of Cultural heritage which create an 
awareness about importance of traditions. 
Department of Art and Design adoptive a 
culture that values collaborative (for art and art 
appreciations) at all levels: across disciplines; 
among faculty, staff and students within 
university or with other universities of region. 
Besides this students and faculty of 
Department carried out joint activities 
(exhibitions, poster campaigns etc.) with 
various AJK Govt. Departments on various 
cultural and social issues.
Collaboration Cultural Celebrations  
their studies. Whether a student need to learn 
'communication tools, develop sculpture, print 
a fabric, or prepare a final project for review; 
the faculty has a resource in place to help. The 
Rota print machine- a traditional printing press 
studio where students have access to state- of 
art equipment including two color printers, 
cutter etc. Students can use camera, 
multimedia and other computer services of 
relevant faculty member for their projects.  
Students seeking academic advice can meet 
with instructors in their offices. Beyond the 
studio, networking opportunities with fellow 
students, faculty, alumni and professional 
flourish through the department of Art and 
Design. Students may also choose from a 
variety of internship opportunities including 
media, fashion and advertising agencies.

=== PAGE 203 ===
Management Sciences
Our primary objective is "to train and educate a class of 
business leaders who will learn cutting-edge technologies 
and practical business skills in a broader intellectual and 
ethical framework to respond to the challenges and 
opportunities of the ever-changing market place".
Merit Based Approach: Department adheres to merit as the 
sole criterion in its decision making. This applies to all aspects 
Our Philosophy
Effective and timely decision-making is key to the success of 
any organization. Management Science supports this 
process through bringing a structured, analytical and rigorous 
approach to decision making. Many organizations looking to 
gain a sustainable competitive edge in an increasingly difficult 
environment use Management Science to deliver improved 
business performance. This will often involve applying 
analytical techniques to the increasing amount of business 
data that is collected. 
Our Vision
Our Objective
The department of management sciences works with every 
student and organization at a deep level, identifying important 
problems and questions, challenging and coaching people to 
find answers, and creating new knowledge. It means we bring 
forward the latest thinking from academia and professional 
practice and apply our combined knowledge to specific 
business situations to turn it into action. Hence, we believe in 
encouraging and supporting people to create new products 
and businesses, pursuing goals for intellectual gain, and 
contributing to social enterprise. 
"Transforming local minds into global minds”
Introduction

professors (TEACHERS) are 
those who unveil the joy of 
learning for students and 
introduce them to a world in 
which anything is possible. We 
have the same faculty who 
works real hard to instill good 
values in us. I would also like to 
appreciate our faculty and 
administration who effectively 
and efficiently works for the 
betterment & success of the 
students. I am glad to have such 
a qualified faculty and to be the 
part of the Managerial Sciences 
department. 
The academic experience and the 
overall student experience at the 
department of management 
sciences has benefited in my 
personal development hugely. It 
has helped me to see the bigger 
picture and become 
independent. The department 
has highly skilled and 
knowledgeable faculty that 
delivers high-quality programs 
committed to the success of its 
students. 
We get to participate in many co-
curricular activities which 
increase our exposure leading to 
improved life skills. 
To be a part of Management 
Sciences department is a matter 
of great pride for me. We have 
highly competent faculty who 
provide us great academic 
insight. Along with that, they 
counsel us in all matters thus 
grooming our personality.

PROSPECTUS 2022-23 221 https://ajku.edu.pk/


=== PAGE 223 ===
The department offers a relatively new field of studies i.e., 
Linguistics along with the traditional and long studied subject 
Literature. A great deal of emphasis is placed on development 
of English Language skills and on producing professionals and 
competent language teachers who are equally conversant with 
English Literature.
Career Prospects
English over many decades has been functioning as a Lingua 
Franca world over and is likely to enjoy this status for another 
100 years. In view of this importance, the career opportunities 
for those graduating in this subject are vast. There is no 
denying the fact that Linguistics and English Language 
Teaching have also gained great deal of importance. The 
Department is fully alive to these modern developments and 
needs and has, therefore, devised a course, which can meet all 
these requirements. The course we offer at BS and Master's 
level is unique and has no parallel in the country. We aim to 
combine all the three subjects in balanced complex where 
every component has an adequate proportion of share. Our 
M.Phil. and Ph.D programs are great amalgam of theory, 
application and research in linguistics. We are rightfully proud 
of pioneering top-tier education in the country. Special 
attention is given to incorporating latest up-to-date trends and 
approaches in the research.
The depth combined with variety makes our graduates 
versatile. Up till now, our graduates have been readily 
absorbed in various fields i.e. teaching, civil services, 
academia, research and higher studies. The market demand is 
still growing. The department is playing a vital role in providing 
trained and competent English teachers to overcome the 
shortage of skilled professionals.
CHAIRMAN        
Dr. Abdul Qadir Khan 
Ph.D (Pak); Post Doc (UK)

Department of English


=== PAGE 224 ===
Total Semesters:    08  
M.Phil English (Linguistics)
In the first two years of the program, the students will 
study compulsory, foundation, and general courses; 
whereas, in years two and three, the students take 
elective/major courses, and in the final year of their 
study, the students are offered specialization in 
Literature or Linguistics.
Program Structure

M.A  English 
Programs
Scheme of Study for B.S English Program
Total Credit hours:   136
 
Total Duration:   4 Years
Eligibility
Ph.D  English (Linguistics)
The criterion for admission to B.S. English is 
intermediate at least in Second Division, and for M.A in 
English is graduation at least in Second Division with 
English Language and preferably English literature. 
For M.Phil/PhD Programs, entry test and University 
GAT (general and subject respectively) are also 
mandatory.
Duration of the Program
Duration of each semester: 16- 18 weeks
The program strictly follows the HEC guidelines and 
specifications for the BS English program. It has 9 
Compulsory Courses of 25 Credit Hours, 7 General 
Courses of 21 Credit Hours, 10 Foundation/Core 
Courses of 30 Credit Hours, 5 Elective courses and 12 
Major courses of 36 Credit Hours. Total 136 Credit 
Hours.

=== PAGE 234 ===
Life at the Department 
Assessment
If you are a linguistics man, or literature savvy person; or 
motivated by general interest in languages, particularly 
English language, UAJK's Department of English is the 
right most choice to satisfy your quest and hone your 
abilities. Friendly milieu, able stewardship and 
exposure to a multitude of national and international 
academic fraternity are the hallmarks of this 
department. Regularly featured International 
Conference on Linguistics every second year, a Y-
category Kashmir Journal of Language Research, local 
and foreign scholarships, international linkages with 
world renowned universities, diverse areas of 
specializations, study tours and extra-mural activities 
are but a few highlights of the department. It is a life 
worth living and experience worth going through. 
The students are assessed through Quizzes, 
Assignments, Presentations, Oral and written 
Examination. The students have to maintain a 
benchmark cumulative score (specified for each degree 
program and may vary from time to time) by the end of 
the degree. The sessional (including Quizzes and 
Assignments) and terminal exams comprise of 50 
marks each.

department and they can discuss anything regarding their educational activities 
with office staff and faculty. 
Hope this department continues to flourish and progress!
UAJK is one of the best and oldest universities in the state of AJK. The multicultural 
environment and capable faculty furnish the students with a solid academic 
foundation and skills for their current responsibilities. This university has embraced 
diversity that ensures a natural talent for leaping over disciplinary borders and 
forging new collaborations. No doubt UAJK provides a congenial environment for 
research and exploration. Further, it also partners with local and international 
community organizations to enhance the students' exposure in different walks of life. 
Hence, some of the best opportunities emerge from the crossroad of knowledge in 
UAJK.

PROSPECTUS 2022-23 234 https://ajku.edu.pk/


=== PAGE 236 ===
The Institute is mandated to promote national and local 
languages spoken in the region. Azad Kashmir is one of the 
richest areas of the world in terms of languages spoken. It is 
a meeting ground for many languages spawning from 
various families. The interaction in these languages gives 
rise to fascinating phenomena which form the basis of 
interesting linguistic studies- a heaven for a linguist. 
Another major aim of the Institutes includes teaching of 
foreign languages which promise bright career 
opportunities in higher studies in the countries they are 
spoken in. The major languages to be taught in the Institute 
include English, Turkish, Arabic, Chinese, French and 
Persian as well as offer on demand language courses 
subject to the availability of the faculty. In the post-
earthquake scenario, it was realized that the ability to 
interact with foreigners in their native languages facilitates 
the work a great deal and is key in fostering warm 
relationships. At the same time, the presence of the 
foreigners and international organizations involved in relief 
and rehabilitation work aroused a good deal of interest in 
foreign languages. In order to implement its agenda, the 
Institute has already run dozens of English language 
courses. It has also entered into collaborations with notable 
international organizations to impart knowledge of 
Chinese, Arabic and Turkish languages for need-based and 
market-oriented purposes. 
As part of Faculty of Arts expansion and realization of the 
importance of knowing more languages in the post-
Earthquake period, the University of Azad Jammu & 
Kashmir decided to establish Institute of Languages.
Yet another aim of the Institute is to offer English support 
program for those who need English skills for effectively 
coping with the degree program or those who want to 
Institute of Languages

The Institute aims to expand in future. Further plans 
includes establishment of a Research Unit which would 
be entirely devoted to promoting research on the local 
languages and preserving the dying languages.
organize seminars and workshops on languages 
teaching, research and computational skills.
The institute has successfully accomplished short 
courses in various languages. In view of Pakistan's 
expanding role in the geopolitical arena and the 
opportunities accompanying, we have decided to 
enable our workforce to cater for the communication 
needs of the opportunity-rich countries. Resultantly, 
language courses of various proficiency levels in 
Chinese, Turkish and English languages are underway. 
We have also hired the services of trained instructors 
through our international collaborators. 
The institute is fully alive to research in linguistics 
particularly targeting the lesser known and marginalized 
local languages. It engages with researchers and 
experts with a view to exploring, promoting and 
preserving local languages. Number of projects aiming 
at documentation and preservation of Pahari, Hindko, 
Kashmiri and Gojri and underway, notable among which 
is compilation of Pahari dictionary and cultural glossary. 
Another undertaking of the Institute is to 
acquire advanced English communicative skills with an 
eye to higher studies abroad or professional 
development. Special courses will also be offered for 
those aiming to take GRE/TOEFL/IELTS tests.
Students, who succeed in the exams at the end of 
each Turkish language course, receive a certificate of 
proficiency in Turkish language indicating their level 
(A1, A2, B1, B2, C1, C2).

Ÿ A.D.E. in-service, later teachers Programs
Teacher-educators play an important role in shaping up the 
life and competence of prospective teachers, it is, therefore, 
essential for them to be adequately equipped with the 
required skills that will enable them to present themselves as 
a perfect role model for the prospective teachers. This 
indicates the importance of effective teaching learning 
systems in teacher education Programs. The Institute of 
Education is one of the pioneering Institutes of Pakistan and 
AJK which offered four years Degree Programs designed as 
per HEC guidelines and while keeping in view the New 
Education Policy 2009, the National Professional Standards 
for Teachers in Pakistan and Accreditation Standards for 
Degree Programs. The Institute also accredited in category 
“Y” by National Accreditation Council for Teacher Education 
(NACTE). The Institute offers:

=== PAGE 259 ===
Cumulative Credits & Modular Programmes
Almost all courses, workshops and short programs are 
designed on credit hour basis. Hence, if you wish to join 
the Institute from off and on for short courses, your 
credits will be accumulated towards a degree or diploma 
or certificate level program. This means that by taking 
admission to that program you may be considered for 
exemption from the courses which you have already 
completed from time to time.
Timings          
Those who have a passion for teaching and are willing to 
take it as their profession and career, then this new B.Ed. 
Hons., degree would be their first choice. This is 
designed as 2+2 year Program. During first two years, 
the students shall study the conventional Bachelor's 
level courses along with some courses in education and 
practical teaching. In the second two years, they will 
study pedagogy oriented content extensively. Hence, 
the students who, due to any compelling personal 
reasons, quit after completing initial two years shall be 
awarded Bachelor Degree in Education. They will be 
better educators especially for private sector that 
presently engages non trained teachers. The Scheme of 
studies for B.Ed (4-Year Program) has been designed in 
accordance with the HEC guidelines and the national 
needs for well-trained teacher educators and education 
managers.
Ÿ The new Accreditation Standards for Education 
Degree Program in Pakistan enforced in August 
2009 which imply that educational institutions shall 
have to improve drastically otherwise these shall not 
be accredited.
Ÿ The Ten (10) National Professional Standards for 
Teachers in Pakistan (NPTSs) enforced in February 
2010. The University of AJK has the Honour of 
pioneering the policy-led change in teacher 
education process and starting 4-year B.Ed. Hons 
Degree in February2010.
Ÿ New Pay scales for those holding 4-years B.Ed. 
(Hons) Degree and two years.
B.Ed. Hons. (4-year)
Unless otherwise explained, all normal Programs are run 
in morning and self-support Programs in evening. On the 
basis of specialized courses, these are usually offered in 
evening and can also be adjusted at weekends.

by the Prime Minister in September 2009. Among other 
features, three dimensions of new policy have led 
responsive universities in Pakistan towards revising and 
improving educational degree awarding systems and in-
service teacher education program. These are;
The new Degree Programmes according to 
new Education Policy (2009)

=== PAGE 261 ===
STRUCTURE OF SCHEME FOR B.Ed. 
(HON'S) ELEMENTARY GROUP
Courses   Credit Hours
Foundation Courses  25
Keeping in view the obsolete nature of the existing BA 
degree in the country, some new sub-disciplines for a BA 
degree are presented below. These Programs contain all 
skill development in selected fields. The students of 
different degree Program can choose any one of the 
following discipline subject to the condition that any 
particular discipline/courses is offered at the time:
    A. Physics, Math    b. Chemistry, Biology
2. Elementary
a. Languages (English-Urdu; English-Arabic; English 
Persian; Urdu-Arabic; Urdu-Persian; Urdu-Kashmiri)
Compulsory Courses  27
This Program has been designed by UAJK keeping in 
view the AJK specific needs and demand-supply gap of 
trained teachers especially in the private sectors that 
shoulders the public sector to a great extent. A large 
number of candidates appear in simple BA exam having 
almost limited use in any professional field. The14 year's 
education gives no benefits to the nation and the 
candidate if he/she has to quit at this stage due to any 
circumstances. Therefore, as mentioned above, to make 
the 4 year education degree more flexible and attractive, 
the degree Program has been split into 2+2 years with 
the provision that the candidate can take exit after 
completing first two years by getting a BA (Education) 
degree or Associate Degree in Education (ADE) and can 
rejoin the Program any time later in the third year within 
five year period.
  The Board of Studies agrees that the faculty of 
education can prepare the course outline for new 
courses, and adopt them subject to approval of the next 
meeting of BOS.

=== PAGE 272 ===
Education as a discipline is relevant to every field of life 
and is considered as a public good. As an academic 
discipline, its purpose is to prepare individuals who 
understand the importance of education as a public 
good. Access to quality education is rightly considered 
as one of the most pressing challenges in the society of 
Azad Kashmir. With very little prospects of 
industrialization in Azad Kashmir due to its controversial 
nature, the avenues for the future employment of our 
youth heavily depend on the quality of education. Even in 
highly developed and industrialized countries, the 
potential for the growth of economies is now considered 
mainly contingent on emerging knowledge. Therefore, 
developed countries are trying to lay the foundations of 
their economies on the basis of knowledge. In the 
context of AJ&K, it becomes imperative to develop our 
human resources through quality education.  However, 
raising the quality of schooling in Azad Kashmir is the 
real challenge. The jobs of today's economy would be no 
more in the recent future and therefore we have to equip 
our current and future generations with skills that are 
relevant to the needs of modern society. To meet such 
challenges and many more, the Institute of Education is 
determined to start Ph.D. program in 2020.
Ÿ · Produce well-trained graduates who are able to 
explore new horizons in the field of education.  
Introduction of Program
Ÿ · Develop a cadre of researchers who would advance 
the knowledge as well as provide localized solutions 
to the challenges in the field of education.
Ph. D in Education Scheme of Studies
Given the ever-increasing importance of schooling, 
there is a great need for a cadre of educational 
researchers who are properly trained in modern 
research methodology and are able to provide the 
research-based solutions to the existing problems of the 
education sector. The aim of this program is to provide a 
strong foundation in both educational theory and 
practice for different specializations in the field of 
education. Ph.D. in education will train emerging 
scholars to serve in a wide variety of challenging 
educational situations. With rigorous training in research 
methods, Ph.D. graduates would be ready to serve 
successfully in careers involving teaching, research, and 
consultancy.
Ph.D. program is expected to:
SCOPE REGARDING MARKET, SOCIAL, 
AND EMPLOYMENT PERSPECTIVE OF 
PROGRAM
Ÿ
Objectives of the Program
Ÿ · Ph.D. program is expected to:
Ÿ · Conduct and disseminate high quality 
research……………
ENTRY REQUIREMENT OF PROGRAM
Ÿ · Global challenges……………..
1. 3.0 CGPA (out of 4.0 in semester system) or First 
division (in Annual system) in M. Phil. / MS or 
equivalent(Research Based) degree in Education
Eligibility Criteria:
2. GAT Subject (Education) by NTSor Departmental 
test (70% passing marks).

Department of Islamic Studies
To be a prestigious, nationally recognized institution offering 
innovative and high quality education on Islam, the Quran and 
on the life of the Prophet Muhammad P.B.U.H with a deeper 
understanding of these fundamentals of life. 
2. To create better understanding of Islam and   
The department of Islamic Studies was launched in Dec. 
2014.The renowned religious and the most influential scholar 
Prof. Qazi Ikram-ur-Rashid became its founding coordinator.
Mission
Introduction
In the name of Allah the most Gracious, the most Merciful. 
Indeed Allah conferred a great favour on the believers when He 
sent among them a Messenger (Muhammad P.B.U.H) from 
among themselves, reciting unto them His Verses (the Quran), 
and purifying them (from sins by their following him), and 
instructing them (in) the Book (the Quran) and Al-Hikmah [the 
worship)], while before that they had been in manifest error (03: 
164).
 
To strive for bringing positive change in the society through 
teaching and researching interfaith and intercultural activities 
from original sources of Islam and to provide quality education, 
skills and behaviour in the fields of Islamic Studies to maintain 
global civilization of love and tolerance through educational 
activism.
Objectives 
1. To establish and promote Islamic education and present  
synthesis of traditional and contemporary Islamic thoughts  
through proper classes and seminars, issuing verdicts  
about religious queries under Ifta Committee.
Vision

Institute of Kashmir Studies
FACULTY: 
Dr. Anbrin Khawaja, Ph.D (PAK)
Dr. Raja Nazakat Ali, Ph. D (IOK)
MS. Shabnum Gull, M.Phil (PAK)

Introduction
The IKS has a close-knit community of interdisciplinary 
scholars that combines expertise in the Languages, 
Literatures, Histories, Archeology, Cultures, Art and Crafts of 
Kashmir. The institute prides itself in its ability to meet a variety 
of research and learning needs. To further enhance and 
promote the quality of teaching and research, the qualified 
permanent, faculty members have been inducted recently. 
The IKS sponsors a variety of  activities including cultural 
events, workshops, conferences, public lectures and other  
programs that promote understanding of the cultures and 
societies of the three regions Gilgit Baltistan, Indian Occupied 
Kashmir and Azad Kashmir  the former State of Jammu and 
Kashmir. Various study tours and visits are regularly arranged 
to expose the students and faculty to different national and 
international conferences/seminars on contemporary issues 
relating to the different fields of society. These extra-curricular 
The institute is committed to ensure quality teaching and 
research in the different areas of Kashmir studies which meet 
all the educational standards of Higher Education 
Commission (HEC). The mission of this institute is to produce 
academically sound scholars to be helpful to resolve the 
challenges relating to the present issues of the society, and 
make them useful and responsible citizens of the society. To 
achieve this mission, the IKS is always devoted to enhance 
students' professional skills and career opportunities. 
The Institute of Kashmir Studies (IKS) was established in 1986 
as a Research Institute in the University of Azad Jammu and 
Kashmir, Muzaffarabad. Since its foundation, the IKS has 
been working to enhance research and development in the 
field of Kashmir Studies. In 2008, postgraduate level degree 
program was started in the IKS which aimed to 
explore and promote the every dimension of Kashmir.

Ÿ BS 4 year Kashmir Studies 
The IKS University of AJK has signed a Memorandum of 
Understanding (MoU) with the Tokyo University of 
Foreign Studies (TUFS) Japan. Under this MoU both 
institutions will develop joint research projects. Through 
Video-Conferencing System, the IKS students will be 
able to attend the online lectures. However, after 
successfully completing this program, IKS students will 
obtain the Certificates from TUFS, Japan. 
In the IKS, seminar is designed to enhance the learning 
capabilities of the students. It encourages reflection 
upon the value and nature of a university education as 
well as helping students to develop strategies for life-
long learning. IKS conducted various seminars for 
students while considering their needs of learning and 
knowledge. Well reputed personalities visited the 
institute and shared their learning and thoughts by 
delivering lectures on various topics related to Kashmir. 
These seminars proved beneficial of enhancement of 
the personal and analytical abilities of the students.
Ÿ M. Phil Kashmir Studies
The program has bright and applied prospectus as it 
provide students great opportunities to join Kashmir 
Liberation Cell, AJK Tourism and Forest Department, 
Kashmir Cultural Academy, NGO's, Political and 
Religious Organizations, Hotel Business, Foreign 
Embassies and Educational Institutes, and further go for 
higher studies in the field of Civil Services, South Asian 
Studies, Regional Studies and etc. It is for the first time 
that Institute is playing a vital role in providing trained and 
competitive teachers for the Progress of the Institution.

BS Political Science (4 Years)

FOR BS (4 YEAR) IN
Duration   4 years
Semester duration  16-18 weeks
The four years BS program in Political 
Science is design to teach the subject of 
Political Science with its multidimensional 
fields. The courses offered in this program are 
included as compulsory and general 
foundational, major and elective courses. The 
compulsory and general courses are included 
from different streams of social sciences 
while the foundational and major courses in 
the fields of political science. This program 
aims for the academic excellence and 
development of an educated society to 
overcome the challenges of contemporary 
world. After studying this course, the students 
will able to get knowledge and understanding 
on comparative political system and 
administrative knowledge for the betterment 
of society and nation at large.

Semester duration  16-18 weeks
The true dissemination of historical 
knowledge is a pre-requisite for a nation's 
development. That is the reason that from 
ancient times, the teaching of History at 
all levels has remained an essential part 
of the curricula of the universities. 
Keeping in view the importance of the 
subject of History, the University of Azad 
Jammu & Kashmir has started BS History 
Program in the Institute of Kashmir 
Studies, which will enable the students to 
learn about Historical legacy of Muslim 
World, and History of struggle for 
Pakistan, socio-political life of Pakistani 
nation, History and culture of Kashmir. 
The courses offered in this program will 
enable the students to connect the 
Historical past with present through 
evaluation and interpretation of Historical 
facts for subsequent better future for 
society. The variety of offered courses 
and focus of this program is to specialize 
the students in field of History with aims to 
develop the understanding on importance 
and role of the subject of History in social 
and academic development.

Ÿ 08 week internship after the completion of 8th 
semester for every student shall be compulsory with

companies, government offices (legal branch), 
NGO's, police stations, legal branch of armed forces, 
stock exchanges, SECP, banks, financial institutions, 
ports, media, political parties, national research 
institutes, industries, and   with other entities to be 
recognized by University/ institution on the 
suggestion of students or faculty. Attachment/ 
internship period spent by each student with any 
entity mentioned hereinbefore shall be assessed on 
the basis of his/her report, self-assessment, faculty 
assessment and assessment provided by 
organizations (Format of Report on internship is 
annexed herewith as Annexure-F).
Ÿ All the Students after the successful completion of

a long dissertation on assigned topics as per policy of 
the University in other BS or Equivalent degree 
programs, however, the similarity index measured 
through Turnitin or other endorsed software up-to 
30% will be allowed.
Ÿ In the last two semesters (IX and X) students can opt 
for four courses (two courses in each semester) of 
their choice from the elective courses offered by the 
university/institution.
Ÿ The candidates also have to clear the grand viva 
( c o m p r e h e n s i v e  o r a l  e x a m i n a t i o n )  o n  
satisfactory/unsatisfactory basis before the panel of 
examiners upon completion of the course work (10 
semesters) to avail the degree. This grand viva shall 
be processed through the procedure provided in 
rules.
Ÿ Internship report shall be evaluated by an oral 
examination on completion of the course work (10 
semesters) through a panel of examiners and the 
candidates have to satisfy the panel to attain the 
degree. This report shall be evaluated through the 
procedure provided in rules.

=== PAGE 316 ===
Extra-curricular activities:
Seminar on "Importance of Vote”
The department of law organized a daylong Seminar for 
the students and faculty/staff of the University aiming at 
disseminating and highlighting the significance of the 
"Right to Vote and Responsibilities of the Voters" on 
April 07, 2016. it further emphasized on strengthening 
the democratic values and choosing the suitable 
representatives for running the affairs of the State. The 
honorable Chief Justice High Court/ Chief Election 
Commissioner AJK Mr. Justice Ghulam Mustafa 
Mughal was the chief guest of the seminar. Other 
speakers were Mr. Justice Ch. Muhammad Ibrahim Zia 
(Justice Supreme Court of AJK),  Mr. Syed Manzoor ul 
Hassain Gilani (Former Chief Justice, AJK), Mr. Raja 
Muhammad Hanif, (Senior Advocate Supreme Court, 
AJK) and Mr. Jawad Faizi (Anchor person PTV). They 
highlighted the significance of the right and duty to vote, 
its historical, legal, Islamic and contemporary 
perspectives and the role of electoral process ensuring 
the wellbeing of the people. Mr. Justice Masood A. 
Sheikh (Justice Supreme Court of AJK), respected 
faculty and other officers of the university also 
participated. Vote of thanks was paid by Professor Dr. 
Khawaja Farooq Ahmed, the Vice Chancellor of the 
University of AJK, Muzaffarabad and the stage was 
conducted by Syed Mudasser Fida Gardazi, Chairman 
Department of Law.
PROSPECTUS 2022-23 315 https://ajku.edu.pk/

=== PAGE 318 ===
The psychology department is dedicated to the education of 
students in enhancing their perception, understanding, and 
appreciating the science of behavior and mental processes at 
both the undergraduate and graduate levels. We endorse the 
educational mission: learn, lead, and change the world. 
Students majoring in psychology are exposed to essential 
concepts that provide them with a solid foundation in the 
development of problem-solving and critical skills which are 
core to our department. Department of Psychology was 
established in 2014 intended to teach academic concepts of 
Psychology in acquaintance with real-world application. The 
psychology department assists students to identify and 
interpret the various mental and emotional factors responsible 
for human psychology.  This program is a baseline for many 
who need to follow higher studies in the field of psychology from 
any recognized university in the world. The students will get a 
chance to discover different fields of psychology like cognitive, 
organizational, environmental, positive psychology, child and 
adolescent psychology, child development, social psychology, 
cross-cultural psychology, and many more. 
Introduction
Vision 
The department of Psychology purposes to impart academic as 
well as functional knowledge of psychology to applicable 
students. The Psychology Department is committed to 
research and graduate training that all underline rigorous 
scientific methods, interdisciplinary collaboration, and a focus 
on the interplay between brain, mind, and behavior. Students 
and faculty establish close alliances to acquire the rational 
principles of performance and mental processes, to make 
meaningful contributions to the science and application of 
psychology, and to produce proficient students who serve the 
native, national, and global communities. 
COORDINATOR  
Dr. Samina Sabir
Ph.D    (Pak)

=== PAGE 324 ===
Department of Sociology & 
Rural Development
Sociology is the scientific study of society. It covers all aspects 
of society like human behavior, social institutions, social 
action, social change and social problems. Sociologists 
investigate structure of groups, organizations and societies 
and the way people interact within these contexts. Since 
human behavior is shaped by social factors, subject matter of 
sociology ranges from intimate family to a hostile mob, from 
the division of society on the basis of race, gender and social 
class to shared beliefs of a common culture and from sociology 
of social work to that of sports. In fact, few fields have such 
relevance and scope for research, theory and application of 
knowledge.
Sociology provides many distinctive perspectives on the 
world, generating new ideas and criticizing old ones. It also 
offers a number of research techniques that can be applied 
virtually to all aspects of social life like emotions, family, 
education, street crimes, delinquency, war and peace, etc.
Sociology is gaining more and more importance with the 
passage of time because  society  is  becoming  more  
complex  and  new  social  problems  are emerging.  Trained  
sociologists  are  the  need  of  the  hour  for  maintaining  the 
smooth functioning of social systems, social life and society.
The Department of Sociology and Rural Development seeks 
to equip the students with sociological knowledge to think 
critically about social world and prepare them for advanced 
education and research.

Program Objectives
Total:                          136 Credit Hours
Sociology is not merely an intellectual discipline; it is also 
a vast profession. Students who study sociology can join 
both government and private sectors in a variety of 
occupations like administration, education, social 
welfare, public health, law, journalism, etc. Sociologists 
can play a rich and diversified role in modern society. 
They can serve in a number of capacities such as 
teacher, researcher, administrator, policy maker, 
planner, consultant, social worker, program evaluator, 
coordinator, clinical counselor, personal manager, etc. 
They can work in areas as broad and diverse as the 
discipline they have chosen.

This program aims to train and nurture experts in the field of 
International Relations and also promote research in this 
important perspective. This Department will play its role as 
a Think Tank and devise and propose research based 
policies to the Governments for effective diplomacy and 
foreign policy in various significant dimensions. One of the 
most important objectives for the establishment of this 
department is to promote research on the conflict of Jammu 
and Kashmir in the modern and emerging trends of the 
Introduction
The Department of International Relations is taking off this 
year to fulfil the need of a state of the art and excellent 
Institution in the field of International Relations in the 
University of Azad Jammu and Kashmir. The Department 
offers a very modern and diverse BS International Relations 
(4 Year) Degree Program to equip the students with latest 
knowledge and train them about emerging trends in the 
field of International Relations. This degree program is very 
important in the wake of the globalization and rapidly 
changing global perspectives affecting the regional and 
local perspectives as a trickle-down effect. 
Whether you want to work in government sector, a global 
company, academia, or a nongovernmental office, a four 
year degree program in International Relations opens 
many doors of opportunities for you. Through our extensive 
range of courses including Contemporary World History, 
International Political Economy, International Law and 
Organization, Diplomacy, Globalization, Foreign Policy, 
Strategic Studies and Regional Economic Integration, you 
will be able to do organized and demanding research and 
will be able to understand a comprehensive details of 
international relations issues and how to develop scholarly 
perspective, as well as apply it. 
Department of International Relations

Coordinator's Message
Welcome to the Department of Electrical Engineering at the University 
of Azad Jammu and Kashmir, Muzaffarabad. It is my real pleasure to 
introduce you to the department. Our mission is to produce first rate 
electrical engineers for the current and long term future needs of 
Pakistan. The technology scenario is dynamic and we are preparing 
our graduates to handle rapid changes in the state of technology. 
Training into self-learning and ability to think are the two fundamental 
requirements for our graduates. The field of electrical engineering was 
built on the shoulders of giants — Tesla, Edison, Marconi, Bell, to 
name a few — to meet the energy and telecommunication needs of 
humanity. Out of our advancements in electronics and photonics 
technology, electrical engineering is keeping the promise. Nowadays, 
most everything we use in our daily lives has a power source and a 
chip and implements some sophisticated communication protocol and 
data/signal processing algorithm. Our faculty and students every day 
define new directions for electrical engineering and strive to develop 
new technologies to help address critical societal challenges. Our 
major areas of research include: Electronics, Telecommunication and 
Power Systems. Students may specialize in these areas, whether it is 
in the form of basic or advanced-level undergraduate courses, 
graduate courses, or research in the lab. The EECS department offers 
PEC accredited undergraduate degrees in electrical engineering. At 
the graduate level, we offer M.S. degree programs in electrical 
engineering with specializations in Electronics, Telecommunication 
and Power. Our undergraduate and graduate curricula are constantly 
refined to meet real-world demands and rapid changes in technology 
to provide students with the skills to address 21st century challenges. I 
invite you to explore our site and learn about our innovative research, 
competitive academic programs, supportive learning environment, 
modern facilities, and global impact. I hope you will explore our 
teaching and research programs and reach out to us with any 
questions. We are always interested in new opportunities for 
collaboration.

Department of Electrical

=== PAGE 355 ===
The vision of the department is “To achieve 
academic excellence by providing quality 
education and conducive research environment 
that craft students for professional development 
and community services” 
Introduction
Vision of Department of Electrical 
Engineering 
The Department of Electrical Engineering was 
established in 2013 and is accredited with the 
Pakistan Engineering Council. Ever since its 
establishment the Department is providing cutting 
edge education to the people of the state of AJ&K 
and Pakistan. The Department has expanded its 
academic activities and offers academic programs 
at various levels. Currently the Department is 
offering academic programs in two main streams of 
Electrical Engineering, Communications and 
Electronics. The academic program is designed to 
build a strong foundation in the fundamentals of 
Electrical Engineering along with specialized 
courses to cover the breadth of the ever expanding 
field of Electrical Engineering. The faculty of the 
Department is highly qualified and is involved in 
active research, both locally and in collaboration 
with national and international research 
organizations. The Department is committed to 
transforming its students into leaders in innovation 
and technology who can manage and shape the 
industry and businesses of today and tomorrow.
Mission of Department of Electrical 
The Department has adopted the Outcome Based 
Education under the umbrella of Pakistan 
Engineering Council.

PEO-2: Exhibit the quest for learning and initiative

Program Educational Objectives (PEOs)
The mission of the Department of Electrical 
Engineering is “To provide requisite knowledge and 
skills that are state of the art and conducive to 
produce competent electrical engineers who can 
contribute towards the betterment of society by 
efficiently fulfilling their professional responsibilities 
in industrial, academic and research organizations.”
Program Learning Outcomes
The graduates of Electrical Engineering program will 
have to attain the following attributes at the end of the 
four-year degree:
Engineering 
PEO-3: Demonstrate commitment to ethical 
practices, community service and societal 
contribution.
1. Engineering Knowledge: An ability to apply 
knowledge of mathematics, science, engineering 
fundamentals and an engineering specialization to 
the solution of complex engineering problems.
4. Investigation: An ability to investigate complex 
engineering problems in a methodical way including 
literature survey, design and conduct of 
experiments, analysis and interpretation of 
experimental data, and synthesis of information to 
derive valid conclusions.
3. Design/Development of Solutions: An ability to 
design solutions for complex engineering problems 
and design systems, components or processes that 
meet specified needs with appropriate consideration 
for public health and safety, cultural, societal, and 
environmental considerations.
2. Problem Analysis: An ability to identify, formulate, 
research literature, and analyze complex engineering 
problems reaching substantiated conclusions using 
first principles of mathematics, natural sciences and 
engineering sciences.

5. Modern Tool Usage:  An ability to create, select 
and apply appropriate techniques, resources, and 
modern engineering and IT tools, including prediction 
and modeling, to complex engineering activities, with 
an understanding of the limitations.
9. Individual and Team Work: An ability to work 
effectively, as an individual or in a team, on 
multifaceted and /or multidisciplinary settings.
11. Project Management: An ability to demonstrate 
management skills and apply engineering principles 
to one's own work, as a member and/or leader in a 
team, to manage projects in a multidisciplinary 
environment.

Particular emphasis is placed on developing 
research potential, fostering and encouraging 
original research and professional competence in the 
field of concentration. 
MS Electrical Engineering
The Department of Electrical Engineering offers a 
selection of graduate courses and activities leading 
to the degrees of Master of Science in Electrical 
Engineering (MSEE). These programs allow 
students to choose a program of study suited to their 
interests, individual needs and talents. 
The program is broad in perspective and maintain a 
balance between scholarly excellence and practical 
relevance. The program is oriented towards 
strengthening the students' background in the area of 
their specialization but are so designed as to deepen 
their understanding in one or more selected areas in 
electrical engineering. 
The Department of Electrical Engineering currently 
offers graduate courses and research activities in a 
variety of areas that span the full breadth of Electrical 
Engineering including communications, modern 
electronics, control and power.

The Department of Software Engineering 
(DSE) is one of two engineering departments 
of UAJ&K Muzaffarabad. The DSE has been 
preparing students for exciting and 
challenging careers not only in Pakistan but 
also in regional and global markets. Students 
are prepared for the workforce with a focus on 
conceptual learning and application. We have 
a national and international level reputation 
for the high quality of our teaching and 
research. The Software Engineering program 
combines research, learning, and practice.

the department and gave the green signal to 
launch the B.Sc. SE program. After the third 
and final visit by the PEC visitation team in 
2022, the department was given Level-1 
Accreditation for Batch 2017-21.

The Mission of The Department of 
Software Engineering
Objectives of Software Engineering 
Program
The B.Sc. Software Engineering program 
at the Department of Software 
Engineering is based on three Program 
Educational Objectives (PEOs). The 
program objective is to produce: 
To prepare graduates to have concrete 
software engineering knowledge, real-world 
problem-solving skills, excellent leadership 
qualities, and entrepreneurial abilities for their 
professional growth and service to society.
UAJ&K's Bachelor of Science in Software 
Engineering curriculum bridges the gap 
between computer science theory and its 
practical applications through the use of 
various tools and technologies. The Software 
Engineering program focuses on providing 
students with practical knowledge and 
technical training that will enable them to 
integrate theory and practice, concepts and 
applications, and problems and solutions. 
This program prepares professionals with 
experience in software development 
concepts, theory, practice, and processes to 
develop high-quality software applications. It 
also produces future change agents, 
individuals who will lead and improve the 
software industry and practices in the next 
years. The graduates of this Program will 
have a strong base in communication, 
mathematics, sciences, cultural, ethical, and 
social issues that influence the development 
of high-quality software systems.
The program educational objectives (PEOs) 
for the Software Engineering program 
describe accomplishments that graduates 
are expected to attain within four years. The 
program enables the students to create high-
quality software engineering systems in a 
systematic, controlled, and efficient manner 
and develops professionals who have a 
mastery of software systems. Graduates of 
the Software Engineering program will be 
able to function as software developers, 
software test engineers, software quality 
assurance engineers, and effective team 
members. They will have ground knowledge 
of communication, mathematics, and 
science. They will have knowledge of and 
experience with software product 
engineering management as well as an 
understanding of professional issues and 
practices.The Software Engineering (SE) 
program at UAJ&K is based on three PEOs 
that are elaborated below:

Welcome to the Faculty of Health and Medical sciences at the UAJK. 
Are you interested in pursuing a career in health care? Congratulation! 
You have made the right decision. We appreciate your interest and will 
help you get through. The need for qualified health professionals is 
enormous in Azad Jammu & Kashmir. After the devastating 2005 
earthquake, healthcare infrastructure has largely been reconstructed 
and renovated. Now, Azad Kashmir enjoys an extensive network of 
modern hospitals and primary health care facilities, perhaps the best 
nationwide. For operating these facilities motivated and enthusiastic 
people like you are required. Through joining our educational 
Programs, you definitely make the right step towards ensuring a 
respectful job. However, working for health is more than a job. It is a 
mission and passion. It will offer you with the self-esteem and 
satisfaction you are searching for. Healthcare career offers numerous 
rewards including making a difference in the lives of patients and 
populations, intellectual challenge and continuous learning, attractive 
compensation and benefits, security and the ability to work anywhere 
in the country. Our educational Programs cover a wide spectrum of 
professions that constitute the backbone of any health facility and the 
key for its success. We have designed our courses in a way that offers 
you the most interactive, informal and encouraging environment 
conducive to learning. You will be encouraged to think critically, to 
seek knowledge yourself and to translate theory into practice. Our 
curricula are based on the art and science of helping you learn. The 
principles of our educational philosophy are that you are self-directed 
learners, have rich experiences and knowledge on which to draw, 
desire to learn to better handle real-life situations, and see education 
as a process that increases competence and leads to achievement of 
your full potential. I am confident that you will excel in our Programs 
and wish you all the best. 
Dr. Bashir ur Rehman

OBJECTIVES

BS Anesthesia Technology 
The Anesthesia Technology program at the Faculty of 
Health and Medical Sciences degree program that offers 
the student an opportunity to develop the technical 
ability, knowledge and skills required for entry level 
employment in any surgical setting, hospital operating 
room or free standing healthcare facility, as a member of 
the anesthesia team
GOAL/AIM
The goal of the course is to prepare anesthesia 
technologist who are highly competent in the knowledge, 
skills and professional demeanor consistent with local 
employer expectations and professional standards.
Ÿ Inducing and maintaining adequate anesthesia
Ÿ Function safely, effectively and efficiently and exhibit 
ethical behavior in an anesthesia technologist role,
Ÿ The specific objectives of the program is to produce 
anesthesia technologist with the capabilities to:
Ÿ Preparing intravenous therapy administration 
equipment
Ÿ Preparing anesthetic monitoring equipment.
Ÿ Checking and setting up the anesthetic machine
Ÿ Monitoring patients' vital signs and anesthesia depth
Ÿ Preparing a range of devices to maintain the patient's 
airway (e.g. laryngeal masks, endotracheal tube)
Ÿ Acquiring transfusion fluids and equipment
Ÿ Collection and analysis of patient (blood) samples
Ÿ Preparing intravenous drugs
Ÿ Undertake efficiently preoperative anesthetic 
procedures, including:
Ÿ Establishing and securing an airway
Ÿ Making sure that patient are positioned in such a way 
not to cause discomfort during their procedure
Ÿ Undertake efficiently intraoperative anesthetics 
procedures, including:
Ÿ Undertake efficiently postoperative anesthetic 
procedures, including:
Ÿ Temperature monitoring and regulation
Ÿ Post operative examination
Ÿ Assessing patient health status,

Introduction
Ÿ Apply scientific principles in the operation and

The curriculum is designed to prepare students to work 
anywhere in Pakistan as highly qualified Cardiovascular 
Technologists.
In the fourth study year students are required to 
complete an eight weeks practicum in an accredited 
institution, attend particular honors seminars, undertake 
independent research and write an honors thesis in a 
major subject.
The Cardiovascular Technology Programme at the 
Faculty of Health and Medical Sciences is a four year 
bachelor of sciences degree Programme that offers the 
student an opportunity to develop the technical ability, 
knowledge and skills required for entry level employment 
in any medical facility as a member of the cardiovascular 
care team.
The goal of the course is to prepare Cardiovascular 
Technologists who are highly competent in the 
knowledge, skills and professional demeanor consistent 
with local employer expectations and professional 
standards. The specific objective of the Programme is to 
produce Cardiovascular Technologists with the 
capabilities to:
Ÿ Complete all work in accordance with legal and 
ethical requirements of the field, using accepted 
safety practices, and appropriate terminology 
specific to the occupation and the industry.
Ÿ Operate sophisticated medical equipment to assist 
physicians in the diagnosis and treatment of 
cardiovascular disorders.
Ÿ Provide competent cardiovascular testing by 
applying comprehensive knowledge of cardiology 
and related disciplines. 
Ÿ Identify emergency equipment, cardiovascular 
drugs, their use and the role of each member of the 
cardiac diagnostic team during a cardiac emergency.
Ÿ Critically evaluate the patient's cardiac status using 
the relevant test procedure and results.

=== PAGE 394 ===
Vision Statement:
Mission Statement:
Our mission is, To emphasis is on the fundamental 
principles and skills required to provide emergency 
medical care for the ill or injured. The primary focus of an 
EMT graduate is to respond to, assess and triage 
emergent, urgent, and non-urgent requests for medical 
care, apply basic knowledge and skills necessary to 
provide patient care and medical transportation to/from 
an emergency or health care facility.
Eemergency Medical Technology program provides the 
student with the necessary knowledge and skills in 
accordance with almost all possible emergency cases 
happening in a hospital. Successful completion of the 
program and upon degree (in Pakistan) and licensure (in 
abroad) the candidate can seek employment with 
Rescue or ambulance service companies, first 
responder agencies, fire departments, and hospitals as 
an Emergency Medical Technologist (EMT). 
To render the Emergency Medical technology Program 
in our state where excellent emergency services are 
immensely required for patients safety management as 
we are deprived of many health care facilities. This 
program should be a leading local and regional model in 
training and teaching ambulatory skills at international 
standards by adopting appropriate teaching methods, in 
line with the modern technological developments. The 
program aims, in this way, to empower students at the 
scientific, academic and cognitive levels for them to keep 
abreast of all that is new in their field.
BS Emergency Medical 
Technology OBJECTIVES:
The goal of this four year bachelor of sciences degree 
program is to prepare Emergency Medical Technologists 
who are highly competent in the knowledge skills and 
professional demeanor consistent with local employer 
expectations and professional standards. The specific 
objective of the program is to produce Emergency 
Medical Technologists with the capabilities to:
ŸDemonstrate knowledge and understanding of medical, 
legal and ethical roles and scope of practice of an 
Emergency Medical Technologist
ŸStrengthen the decision power and exercise 
appropriate judgment skills, to be applied especially 
during crisis.
ŸUse mobile equipment for monitoring of patient's vital 
signs and electrocardiography during transportation. 
ŸTransport patients to hospital safely and quickly.
ŸFunction safely, effectively and efficiently and exhibit 
ethical behavior in an emergency medical role.
ŸDrive ambulance vehicles safely and efficiently to the 
emergency scene.
ŸApply effectively and safely procedures for hemorrhage 
control and fracture and spinal Immobilization / 
Management. 
ŸApply procedures for basic and advanced life support. 
ŸAdminister drugs and fluids orally and parentally 
following strict protocols for life saving of patients in the 
life threatening situations. 
ŸApply basic biomedical scientific principles for safe 
management of medical and surgical emergencies at 
pre hospital care level. 
·Carry out critical care procedures during transportation 
PROSPECTUS 2022-23 390 https://ajku.edu.pk/


=== PAGE 395 ===
The curriculum is designed to prepare students to work 
anywhere in Pakistan and abroad as highly qualified 
Emergency Medical Technologist. In the regard, the 
curriculum meets international standards in ETMT 
certification and ensures that students master world class 
performance competencies. Subject are taught over eight 
semesters the duration of each semester is 18 weeks. A 
Maximum of 24 Credits can be obtained in a single 
semester. The Bachelor of Science in Emergency Medical 
Technology is awarded when the student accumulates a 
total of 124 credits.

significant others colleagues and other members of the 
healthcare team.
In the fourth study year students are required to complete 
an eight weeks practicum in an accredited institution 
attend particular honors seminars undertake independent 
research and write an honors thesis in a major subject.

The curriculum is designed to prepare students to work 
anywhere in Pakistan as highly qualified Medical Lab 
Technologist. In this regard, the curriculum meets 
international standards for certification and ensures that 
students masters world-class performance 
competencies.
Ÿ Maintain accuracy in the performance of tests and 
eliminate error through recognizing irregularities and 
making corrections
Ÿ Maintain the confidentiality of patient results,
The goal of the course is to prepare Medical Laboratory 
Technologists who are highly competent in the 
knowledge, skills and professional demeanor consistent 
with local employer expectations and professional 
standards. The specific objectives of the Program is to 
produce Medical Laboratory Technologists with the 
capabilities to:
Ÿ Cooperate with their coworkers and all members of 
the medical team.
Ÿ Take responsibility for their own work and organize 
their work to make the most efficient use of time,
Ÿ Adapt well to various work situations,
Ÿ Perform efficiently under stress,
Ÿ Perform competently the tests routinely performed in 
clinical laboratories.
Subjects are taught over eight semesters, the duration of 
each semester is 20 weeks. A maximum of 18 credits can 
be obtained in a single semester. The Bachelor of 
Science in Medical Laboratory
Introduction
Technology is awarded when the student accumulates a 
total of 130 credits. In the fourth study year students are 
required to complete an eight weeks practicum of 
Medical Laboratory technology in an accredited 
institution, attend particular honors seminars, undertake 
independent research and write an honors thesis in a 
major subject.
Ÿ Function safely, effectively and efficiently and exhibit 
ethical behavior in Medical laboratory Technologist 
role.
BS Clinical Laboratory Sciences
Ÿ Demonstrate and explain routine procedures to 
others,
Ÿ Keep accurate and legible records and communicate 
reports clearly to fellow medical personnel,
Ÿ Master a working knowledge of the principles of the 
tests they are performing,
The Laboratory Medical Technology Program at the 
Faculty of Health and Medical Sciences is a four-year 
Bachelor of Sciences degree Program that offers the 
student an opportunity to develop the technical ability, 
knowledge and skills required for entry level employment 
in any healthcare facility, as a laboratory medical 
technologist.
Ÿ Collect, label, identify, and log in specimens 
accurately,
Ÿ Correlate test results in order to confirm them.
Ÿ Participate in professional organizations in their 
specialty,

Hence, Physical therapy covers basic parameters of healing 
sciences i.e. preventive, promotive, diagnostic, rehabilitative, and 
curative.
Program Vision:  
Program Mission:
Physical therapy is an essential segment of modern health care 
system. It is a "science of healing and art of caring". It pertains to 
the evaluation, assessment and treatment of musculoskeletal, 
Neurological, Cardio-Vascular and Respiratory syste ms' 
functional disorders including symptoms of pain, ed ema, 
physiological, structural and psychosomatic ailments. It also deals 
with methods of treatment based on movement, manual therapy, 
physical agents, and therapeutics modalities to relieve the pain 
and other complications.
Doctor Of Physical Therapy program aims to impart evidence-
based theoretical, practical and clinical knowledge and skill 
students to achieve the highest level of morality and creativity 
through shared stimulating experiences and transform them into 
competent health professional 
Vision is to be a model program focused on the enhancement and 
nourishment of an academic environment conducive to excellence 
in teaching research and community services. And to be a 
rehabilitative science institute of national and international repute 
known in quality and value-based education 
1. Demonstrate in-depth evidence-based knowledge of the 
basic and clinical sciences relevant to physical therapy, 
both in their fundamental context and in their application

Our two years MPH program is designed to provide excellence in 
teaching of various subjects regarding preventive health sciences, 
environmental and occupational health hazards, planning and 
policy making and statistical methods and concepts with special 
emphasis on practical application of the knowledge and methods in 
Public Health and clinical settings. It is oriented towards enabling 
the student to meet a set of international technical competencies 
and employability criteria. Our graduates can compete for jobs 
nationally and internationally. In doing so, we shifted the emphasis 
in the curriculum design from general theoretical inputs to place it 
on competency and outcome based programming.  
Dear Students
I welcome you whole heartedly to the Faculty of Health and Medical 
Sciences at the University of Azad Jammu and Kashmir. Public 
Health is concerned with generating knowledge about preventing 
disease, promoting health and prolonging life. Faculty of Health 
and Medical Sciences is the first ever public sector effort of the 
state of Jammu and Kashmir to offer and launch two year MPH 
program. 
We have designed the program in a way that offers you the most 
interactive, informal and encouraging environment conducive to 
learning. You will be encouraged to think critically, to seek 
knowledge yourself and to translate theory into practice. Our 
curriculum is based on the art and science of helping you learn. The 
principles of our educational philosophy are that you are self-
directed learners, have rich experiences and knowledge on which 
to draw, desire to learn to better handle real-life situations, and see 
education as a process that increases competence and leads to 
achievement of you full potential. Last but not least, every facility 
that makes you enjoy learning shall be at your disposal. I am 
confident that you will excel in this program and wish you all the 
best.

Goal of the MPH Program
The MPH program offered by UAJK aims to improve the 
health status of the population, which is to be achieved 
by providing public health and health care professionals 
with a high quality postgraduate training program in 
public health sciences.
Objectives of the MPH Program
The MPH Program has 60 credit hours. A total of 54 
credit hours are accumulated in three semesters and 
the fourth (final) encompassing six credit hours of 
thesis. Each module in 1-3 semesters will be of three 
(03) credit hours each culminating in a total of 18 credit 
hours. The fourth (final) semester encompassing thesis 
work will be of six (06) credit hours.

=== PAGE 429 ===
NEELUM CAMPUS

=== PAGE 430 ===
Keran & Neelum
Neelum is located at a distance of about 9 km from 
Athmuqam town on the right bank of the river Neelum at 
the altitude of 1524 meters above sea level. With its 
fascinating sceneries the panoramic lush green valley 
abounds in fruit and wildlife. The picturesque village 
facing the Line of Control (LoC) offers a captivating view 
to the beholder.
About 240 kilometers long picturesque Neelum Valley is 
situated to the North and North East of Muzaffarabad. 
Running parallel to the Kaghan Valley, it is separated 
from it by snow covered peaks, over 4000 meters above 
sea level. The district Neelum happens to be the largest 
district of AJK with an area of 3621 sq. kms; spread from 
Noseri to the Gurez valley in Taobut. Excellent scenic 
beauty, panoramic view, towering hills on both sides of 
the noisy Neelum river, flourishing green forests, 
streams, high altitude lakes and attractive surroundings 
make the valley an ideal tourist resort. The area is also 
suitable for mountain tourism. A part of Nanga Parbat 
Massif falls in this area which is dominated by the 
"Sarvali Peak" (6326 meters) the highest mountain in 
Azad Kashmir. Moreover like the Kaghan Valley in the 
Khyber Pakhtunkhwa, Neelum is famous for fishing and 
angling activities in the river Neelum and the Jaghran 
Nullah which are stocked with trout fish. A fair weather 
road opens the valley for tourists up to Kel (155 
Kilometers from Muzaffarabad). Buses ply daily on this 
route and accommodation facilities are also available at 
places of tourist attraction.
Dawarian is about 22 kms from Athmuqam. This lush 
hilly village also leads to a well-known lake at the 
boundary of Dawarian and Kaghan Valley. This village 
has high literacy rate of around 80%. The panoramic 
lush green valley is rich in fruit and wildlife.
Athmuqam
Kel
Dawarian
Sharda
Sharda is a splendid green valley spread around the 
river Neelum at an elevation of 1981meters above sea 
level. Shardi and Nardi are two mountain peaks 
overlooking the valley, reputedly named after a 
legendary princess Sharda. It has a captivating 
landscape with numerous springs and hillsides covered 
with trees. Sharda is also famous for centuries old (5000 
B.C) ruins of Buddhist University offering great 
prospects to historians and archaeologists.
Athmuqam is situated at a height of 1371 meters above 
sea level and is the district headquarter of the Neelum 
district. It is an attractive place known for its variety of 
fruits. All necessary basic facilities viz. bazaars and 
markets, post offices, banks, hospitals, hotels and 
telephone exchanges are available in the town. 
Athmuqam leads further to greater sites of tourist 
attraction such as Keran/Neelum, Dowarian, Lawat, 
Sharda, Surgan, Kel, Shunter, Aran Kel, Helmet, Gurez 
and Taobut.
Kel is a small town at a height of 2097meters. This is 
another picturesque place in the Neelum Valley. It 
further leads to other marvelous sites such as, Aran Kel, 
Shounter Pass, Helmet, Gurez Valley and Taobut. It 
also borders the region of Gilgit-Baltistan (G&B).
NEELUM VALLEY: A Brief Overview
PROSPECTUS 2022-23 426 https://ajku.edu.pk/


=== PAGE 431 ===
The Neelum campus was established in July 2013. The 
establishment of the Campus is directed at providing 
higher education to the people of the Neelum Valley, so 
as to bring them into the main stream through education, 
to inculcate in them the spirit of global citizenship and to 
prepare them to harmonize under global socio-cultural 
diversity. The Campus provides ample opportunities of 
higher education to the students of Neelum Valley in 
particular. It aims at equipping the students with 
teachings and research in the fields of sciences, 
technologies and humanities. The campus envisions to 
make the Neelum Valley an educational hub besides its 
being a tourists destination of choice and to revive the old 
monumental tradition of old (5000 B.C) great Buddhist 
University at Sharda as it had been famous for centuries. 
The Campus has a well-furnished building with a 
computer laboratory, library, and a conference hall. The 
campus has the hostel facility for non-local students. A 
spacious play ground is available for sports besides an 
indoor hall for indoor games. It is hoped that the Neelum 
campus of UAJK will prove to be a great contributing 
factor in the socio-economic uplift of the area.
The Neelum Campus at Athmuqam
PROSPECTUS 2022-23 427 https://ajku.edu.pk/

Almost every aspect of modern life involves computing - 
from mobile phones, TVs, planes, trains and cars, to 
gaming, security and medicine. Every organization's 
payroll, customer records and finances are held 
electronically, sometimes containing millions and 
millions of records. The digital world is really upon us. 
Looking to the future, initiatives such as cloud computing 
will connect us all through small portable devices, 
enhancing social networking and allowing us to 
exchange audio and video data from our daily lives.
The Department of Computer Sciences and Information 
Technology at Neelum campus was started in 2013 with 
the start of this new campus. For Computer Scientists 
the current era is full of excitement and challenges as the 
discipline is now widely recognized to be an essential 
source of tools and techniques for advancements in 
nearly all spheres of human endeavor. Computer 
Science is a fascinating subject, and one that is now 
indispensable in our lives.
Introduction of DCS & IT
Keeping in mind the absolute dependence of the 
contemporary world requirements over Information 
technology the program offered by the department is 
tailored towards producing computer scientists capable 
of achieving new heights in innovation and enhancement 
of technology to provide the world an even better 
tomorrow. Our program is accredited by the Higher

strictly follow the standard curriculum guidelines as laid 
down by the HEC.

Department of Geology
Introduction
Geology is the science comprising the study of solid Earth, the 
rocks of which it is composed, and the processes by which they 
change. Geology can also refer generally to the study of the solid 
features of any celestial body. Neelum is full of natural resources. 
Excellent scenic beauty, panoramic view, towering hills on both 
sides of the noisy Neelum River, flourishing green forests, 
streams, high altitud e lakes and attractive surroundings make the 
valley a dream come true. The area is also ideal for mountain 
tourism. Different types of minerals and precious stones are 
available in this valley. Keeping in view of the importance of the 
area, BS Program in Applied Geology will be started in this 
session. The departmentwill be unearthing and exploiting the 
natural resources of the country is blessed with and by doing so, it 
will be playing an important role in socio-economic uplift of the 
country.
The internship facilities will be provided for final year graduates. Students will be sent to Oil and Gas companies, 
Geotechnical Organizations, WAPDA, Geological Survey, FWO, OGDC, PPL, POL and other industrial organizations.

=== PAGE 443 ===
Adding to the sphere of UAJK's operation and in view of the educational needs of the inhabitants of the Jehlum Valley, 
Jhelum Valley Campus has been established on August 01, 2015 at Hattian Bala. The campus has been initially housed in 
the building of Boys Inter College Hattian Bala along the Srinagar-Muzaffarabad Highway. In the first phase, three 
undergraduate degree programs have been launched in the disciplines of English, Education and Computer Sciences.
Jhelum Valley Campus
The cultural norms of the area and lack of resources limit the higher education opportunities for the youth especially 
females who are unable to join higher education institutions in big cities. The university campus at Jhelum Valley is 
providing the higher education facilities to the youth at their doorsteps and enables them to take part in national 
development.
PROSPECTUS 2022-23 439 https://ajku.edu.pk/

Department of Computer Sciences 
& Information Technology 
Undergraduate Computer Sciences 
Programs
The Department of CS&IT offers four years BS (CS) 
program at undergraduate level. The program in 
computer science prepares professionals who will 
adapt to constant changes in technology and who will be 
leaders in developing the new technologies of the 
Information Age. The multidisciplinary nature of our 
curricula offers students a high degree of flexibility to 
design a program of study tailored to their interests and 
professional aspirations.
Computer Sciences focuses on modeling, analysis, 
documenting and tracking the system requirements, 
designing, implementing, deployment, and maintaining 
software systems.  Moreover, programming, 
algorithms, and the principles of computing that underlie 
these areas are addressed. Computer Sciences 
program also assures to produce graduates that are 
prepared either to begin careers or to pursue advanced 
studies in the field.
Introduction
The Department of Computer Science and Information 
Technology (CS&IT) has been established in August, 
2015, University of Azad Jammu and Kashmir, Jhelum 
Valley Campus. 
The mission of the Department of Computer Science 
and Information Technology is to provide high quality 
education at undergraduate level, to prepare computer 
science professionals and experts for productive 
careers in academia, industry and Government, by 
providing the conducive learning and research 
environment in the theoretical and applied domains. 
The Department has modern computing labs and class 
rooms with multimedia facility. The Department is 
connected through a LAN in order to provide modern 
teaching and learning environment and fast access to 
the Internet. 
Job Opportunities for Graduates
Students graduating from the Department are well 
prepared for employment in a wide range of high-tech 
companies and able to help maintain and enhance the 
AJ & K's position in this important and expanding area. 
The Higher Education Commission recommended BS 
programs is aimed at a closely related set of 
technologies in order to provide its graduates with upto-
date technical knowledge, development and research 
skills, together with an appreciation of industrial 
perspectives. Ÿ Depth and breadth of knowledge in Computer 
Sciences as evidenced by an understanding of 
computing systems and science coupled with the 
capacity to produce feasible and responsible 
solutions to complex computing problems.

Sciences Programs

=== PAGE 457 ===
ET TO TG H EO  T U AW JKOH  
MUZAFFARABAD
Starting from Islamabad, you should follow the 
Kashmir Road to Murree till you reach Kohala and 
Muzaffarabad. In Muzaffarabad take the Neelum 
Road to reach the Chehla Campus after crossing the 
Chehla Bridge over river Neelum.
King Abdullah Campus, Chatter Klass 
Starting from Islamabad, you should follow 
the Kashmir Road to Murree till you reach 
Kohala and Chatter Klass. 
About 200 kilometers long, the picturesque Neelum Valley is 
situated to the North & North East of Muzaffarabad. Running 
parallel to the Kaghan Valley, Neelum Valley is 155 kilometers 
from Muzaffarabad, of which about 150 kilometers road has been 
black topped while the remaining portion is being metaled.
NEELUM VALLEY (ATHMUQAM)
JHELUM VALLEY (HATTIAN BALA)
This is an ideal valley to visit for both the domestic and international 
tourists. The curling river Jhelum passes through from east to west 
between the high green mountains of this valley and joins the river 
Neelum at Domel near Muzaffarabad city. A 59 Kilometers long 
metal road runs along the river Jhelum from Muzaffarabad to 
Chakothi, which is located adjacent to the Line of Control (LOC). 
Buses & wagons ply this route regularly while it is also used as 
Muzaffarabad Srinagar Trade, route.
DISTRICT JHELUM VALLEY
